# Actor_B animation graph export report

Generated by `tools/anim_graph/export_actor_b_graph.py` (schema 1, resolver `asc_once`). Do not edit by hand.

**Authorization.** owner-stated; see Player_Animation_Project/ASSET_AUTHORIZATION.md; granting party and terms not on file.

**Fidelity policy.** graph-authored behaviour is the default; gameplay deviations are opt-in flags (gameplay_overrides, all default false). The only default-on deviations keep a behaviour Frontier has today (user requirement 2) and are listed in resolver_exceptions, each with the graph result exported beside it.

## Thrown weapons (throw chain)

The graph's aimed throw chain for the thrown-weapon profiles (throwables_design.md 3.2): aim-idle / aim-run -> (throw-object-aim, the 0.2 s raise, from aim-run) -> throw-object-aim-start (release-start, 0.2 s) -> auto at phase 1, fade 0 -> throw-object-aim-end (release-end, 0.6 s) -> at phase 0.8 throw-object-aim^idle (back to carry) or throw-object-aim^aim (back to aim; no Actor_B clip), with the movement / crouch exits of both. Each state has one leaf, resolved per profile and stance like the other roles.

Release frame: 0.05 s into `end` at 1x (tests/throwables/survey_throw_clips.gd: the right hand is fastest (10.2-11.5 m/s standing, 8.8 crouched) in release-end's first key interval; the object leaves 0.05 s into release-end at 1x (0.25 s into the 0.8 s start + end chain, Throwable.RELEASE_PHASE). The graph has no release event state (its +0x2c callback i32[D0+156] is a per-state runtime integer), so the game supplies it: PlayerAnim calls Throwable.release_from_animation()).

## Frontier carry overrides (THROW2)

THROW2 (user playtest 2026-09-25): carries of thrown weapons the user wants held unlike the graph's pick-up weapon base (its 1h carry arm, p-1h-arm--part, holds an object up in front of the chest). A row replaces the weapon-base layers of its `replace` slots on every lower-body role of its modes that carries such a layer (idle, direction banks, starts, stops, reversals, turns; air, landing, hit and death roles carry no weapon-base arm layer), except `roles_except` (and, when given, only `roles_only`), with (1) `from_profile`: the weapon-base layers of those slots that another graph profile resolves on the same role (its records and lambda weights; a target with no Actor_B action takes `substitute`, a role that profile has no record on takes its idle role's), then (2) `poses`: [pose, weight] pairs, each an Actor_B clip sampled at one key time (`sample_s`) on a bone mask, baked by TreeBuilder (the clip's asset ids and attribution are kept). Rows apply in order; a later row replaces an earlier row's layers. Layer status: 'frontier' (pose), 'substitute', 'resolved' (the other profile's graph layer); every role lists its rows in `carry_override` with the layers they replaced. Unchanged: the throw chain, the aim trees and their grips (the wound-up pose, release and follow-through), and the draws (action_overrides changes the tomahawk's).

## Crouch change

Full body plays by default (`options.crouch_change_full_body` = true): graph default (on): a crouch toggle takes the graph's first-match record into the full-body state crouch-change-idle^idle (0.2 s) from every locomotion state. Its resolved clip (stand^crouch / crouch^stand) has no Actor_B action, so it plays the Actor_B full-body pair actor_b-p-stand^crouch--base / actor_b-p-crouch^stand--base (status substitute; same 1.0 s family as the resolved crouch-change additives). Off: the rows' fallback records (change-move-mode self re-entry, 0.5 s) plus the additive, the only path that needs no substitute clip

- atlatl/crouch_to_stand: full body actor_b-p-crouch^stand--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-crouch^stand--add (resolved)
- atlatl/stand_to_crouch: full body actor_b-p-stand^crouch--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-stand^crouch--add (resolved)
- bow/crouch_to_stand: full body actor_b-p-crouch^stand--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-crouch^stand-rifle--add (resolved)
- bow/stand_to_crouch: full body actor_b-p-stand^crouch--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-stand^crouch-rifle--add (resolved)
- musket/crouch_to_stand: full body actor_b-p-crouch^stand--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-crouch^stand-rifle--add (resolved)
- musket/stand_to_crouch: full body actor_b-p-stand^crouch--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-stand^crouch-rifle--add (resolved)
- pistol/crouch_to_stand: full body actor_b-p-crouch^stand--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-crouch^stand-pistol--add (resolved)
- pistol/stand_to_crouch: full body actor_b-p-stand^crouch--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-stand^crouch-pistol--add (resolved)
- spear/crouch_to_stand: full body actor_b-p-crouch^stand--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-crouch^stand--add (resolved)
- spear/stand_to_crouch: full body actor_b-p-stand^crouch--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-stand^crouch--add (resolved)
- tomahawk/crouch_to_stand: full body actor_b-p-crouch^stand--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-crouch^stand--add (resolved)
- tomahawk/stand_to_crouch: full body actor_b-p-stand^crouch--base (substitute), additive actor_b-p-stand^crouch--add (resolved)

## Resolver disagreements

One overlay loop, asc_once, resolves every leaf and layer target (bindings.resolver_policy, with the graph evidence in resolver_policy). The engine's loop is not in the data: asc_fix repeats ascending passes until stable, asc_once restarts from the lowest layer after each hit. The two agree on every exported leaf except the ones in resolver_disagreements; those roles are marked resolution 'ambiguous' and keep the other result in resolver_alternatives. resolver_exceptions lists the leaves that use the other loop anyway. Each one keeps a behaviour Frontier has today (user requirement 2); it is not a claim about the game's loop, and the graph result stays exported beside it.

Export loop `asc_once` (compared with `asc_fix`): One overlay loop for every leaf. The engine's loop is not in the data, but the authored records only make sense under asc_once (restart from the lowest-priority layer after every hit): under asc_fix the whole crouch bow-draw family is dead, and asc_once treats the long-gun weapon-out arms in crouch the same way whether they are reached as a layer target or as a substitution. The two loops give the same result on every other exported leaf and layer target.

- crouch-crouch 0x0001ba58 (tense-bow-draw -> crouch-bow-draw) and 0x0001ba6c (#35686a63 -> #bbd66620), and actor_b 0x00003e60 / 0x00003e74 (-> actor_b-p-crouch-bow-reload--part / --add): under asc_fix no state leaf and no layer target reaches them in any of the 12 weapon x mode selections, because tense-bow-draw / #35686a63 exist only as weapon-base-bow outputs (0x00004df8 / 0x00004de4) and actor_b (priority 37) consumes them in the same ascending pass before crouch (priority 9) is revisited. Under asc_once they are live exactly for the bow's crouch reload (tests/test_export.py test_resolver_policy_evidence).
- idle-gunout-1lg2h-arms in crouch: as a layer target (weapon-base-long-gun flags 01 records such as 0x0000a508) it resolves through crouch 0x0001b9e0 and actor_b 0x00003974 to null under both loops; only asc_once gives the same null when it is reached from 1p-idle-fingers (weapon-base-long-gun 0x00009cac), while asc_fix lets actor_b 0x000039b0 consume it first (actor_b-p-rifle-idle-hands).

| where | asc_fix | asc_once | used | basis | why |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| actions.bow/crouch/nock.partial | actor_b-p-bow-reload--part | actor_b-p-crouch-bow-reload--part | asc_once | resolver_policy | export loop asc_once (bindings.resolver_policy) |
| actions.bow/crouch/nock.twin | actor_b-p-bow-reload--add | actor_b-p-crouch-bow-reload--add | asc_once | resolver_policy | export loop asc_once (bindings.resolver_policy) |
| hand_states.musket/crouch.weapon_out | actor_b-p-rifle-crouch-idle--hands | null | asc_fix | frontier_requirement | Not a claim about the game's loop. Under the export loop (asc_once) this leaf follows crouch 0x0001b9e0 into actor_b 0x00003974 (-> null), which disables the musket's weapon-out finger partial in crouch. Nothing then drives r_hand_prop_attachment at full weight in most musket crouch roles (crouch locomotion at 0.8 from rifle-crouch-run-arms; idle turns, takeoff, jump, several landings and death at 0; the export counts them in meta.resolver_exceptions). Measured in Godot 4.7, the rifle grip keys (rifle-crouch-run-arms, rifle-crouch-idle--hands) put that bone 4.2 cm and 36 deg from its rest pose, so the musket would visibly twist in the hand. To keep today's behaviour this one leaf uses the asc_fix result actor_b-p-rifle-idle-hands (Godot action actor_b-p-rifle-crouch-idle--hands). The graph result (null) is kept in resolver_alternatives; set enabled to false to export it, and (g) then exempts the listed roles. |

Resolver exceptions (the other loop on purpose, to keep a behaviour Frontier has today):

- `hand_states.musket/crouch.weapon_out` uses asc_fix instead of the export loop asc_once (user requirement 2: weapons stay attached to the hand prop bones and placed by the game's --hands / carry --arms clips). Graph result: null (disabled); used: actor_b-p-rifle-crouch-idle--hands. Roles whose only full-weight prop driver this choice supplies: 40. Disable it in bindings.resolver_choices to export the graph result; (g) then exempts `roles.musket/crouch/*`, `runtime_roles.hit_full.musket/crouch/*`, `runtime_roles.death.musket/crouch/*`.

## Weapon layers and prop bones

- 1. lower-body role clip (roles.<w>/<m>/<role>)
- 2. that role's layers, in listed order (ascending overlay priority, then record offset; INFERRED), including move-set finger layers such as actor_b-p-sprint-fingers / actor_b-fist-caut-hands
- 3. weapon partial slot: the weapon-out finger state (weapon_layers.weapon_out; musket and pistol, see apply_rule) while no action plays; an action's partial (actions.*.partial: draw, holster, grab, nock, reload) replaces it in the same slot
- 4. weapon additive slot: the action's twin (actions.*.twin)
- 5. runtime additives: fire, hit_add, charge

Weapon-out fingers (`options.weapon_out_fingers`, state `idle-pistol-out-part` 0x00004de8, entered by code (no incoming transition record in the whole graph), default on: true, weight 1.0: INFERRED: idle-pistol-out-part is a code-entered partial state with no weight in the data; played at 1). lower-body roles (roles.*, runtime_roles.hit_full/death) whenever a weapon is out, as a looping partial after all of the role's layers (step 3 of order), for the weapons it applies to (weapon_out.clips[*].applies). It is faded out by the aim weight: aim_trees carry their own hands layer (aim_trees.<w>/<s>.hands), which a full-weight weapon-out partial would hide (INFERRED).

Applies when: The partial applies to a weapon only when that weapon's weapon-base set remaps the state's leaf 1p-idle-fingers (long-gun 0x00009cac, pistol 0x00019368). weapon-base-bow has no such record: the leaf falls through to actor_b's generic actor_b-p-hands (0x00000328), and the bow's fingers come from its state-keyed actor_b-p-bow-hands layers on the locomotion states, which a full-weight generic partial played after them would hide. Order, weight and trigger stay INFERRED; WP5 should confirm the bow from screenshots and can set force.bow.

| weapon/stance | weapon-out clip | applies | why | keys prop bones | resolution |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| atlatl/crouch | actor_b-fist-caut-hands | false | weapon-base-pick-up has no 1p-idle-fingers remap: the leaf falls through to actor_b's generic finger clip, which would hide the weapon's own hands layers (apply_rule) | - | unique |
| atlatl/stand | actor_b-fist-caut-hands | false | weapon-base-pick-up has no 1p-idle-fingers remap: the leaf falls through to actor_b's generic finger clip, which would hide the weapon's own hands layers (apply_rule) | - | unique |
| bow/crouch | actor_b-fist-caut-hands | false | weapon-base-bow has no 1p-idle-fingers remap: the leaf falls through to actor_b's generic finger clip, which would hide the weapon's own hands layers (apply_rule) | - | unique |
| bow/stand | actor_b-fist-caut-hands | false | weapon-base-bow has no 1p-idle-fingers remap: the leaf falls through to actor_b's generic finger clip, which would hide the weapon's own hands layers (apply_rule) | - | unique |
| musket/crouch | actor_b-p-rifle-crouch-idle--hands | true | weapon-base-long-gun remaps 1p-idle-fingers (0x00009cac) | l_hand_prop_attachment, r_hand_prop_attachment | ambiguous (asc_fix, frontier_requirement) |
| musket/stand | actor_b-p-rifle-crouch-idle--hands | true | weapon-base-long-gun remaps 1p-idle-fingers (0x00009cac) | l_hand_prop_attachment, r_hand_prop_attachment | unique |
| pistol/crouch | actor_b-r-hand-revolver | true | weapon-base-pistol remaps 1p-idle-fingers (0x00019368) | r_hand_prop_attachment | unique |
| pistol/stand | actor_b-r-hand-revolver | true | weapon-base-pistol remaps 1p-idle-fingers (0x00019368) | r_hand_prop_attachment | unique |
| spear/crouch | actor_b-fist-caut-hands | false | weapon-base-pick-up has no 1p-idle-fingers remap: the leaf falls through to actor_b's generic finger clip, which would hide the weapon's own hands layers (apply_rule) | - | unique |
| spear/stand | actor_b-fist-caut-hands | false | weapon-base-pick-up has no 1p-idle-fingers remap: the leaf falls through to actor_b's generic finger clip, which would hide the weapon's own hands layers (apply_rule) | - | unique |
| tomahawk/crouch | actor_b-fist-caut-hands | false | weapon-base-pick-up has no 1p-idle-fingers remap: the leaf falls through to actor_b's generic finger clip, which would hide the weapon's own hands layers (apply_rule) | - | unique |
| tomahawk/stand | actor_b-fist-caut-hands | false | weapon-base-pick-up has no 1p-idle-fingers remap: the leaf falls through to actor_b's generic finger clip, which would hide the weapon's own hands layers (apply_rule) | - | unique |

- idle-pistol-out-part (reference clip 1p-idle-fingers) has no incoming transition record: the game code enters it. It is the only looping state of the weapon partial group; idle-hands is its no-weapon counterpart. That it plays whenever a weapon is out is INFERRED from the state name and structure.
- pistol: weapon_out resolves to actor_b-r-hand-revolver, the only clip that keys r_hand_prop_attachment on pistol locomotion (r-arm-gun keys 6 arm bones, sprint-fingers keys no prop bone).
- musket: weapon_out resolves to actor_b-p-rifle-idle-hands (Godot action actor_b-p-rifle-crouch-idle--hands) standing. In crouch the export loop (asc_once) disables it (actor_b 0x00003974 -> null); the asc_fix result is used there by bindings.resolver_choices (user requirement 2, see meta.resolver_exceptions).
- bow: not applied (apply_rule). The leaf would resolve to the generic actor_b-p-hands (Godot action actor_b-fist-caut-hands), which keys palm and finger bones but no prop bone and would override the authored state-keyed actor_b-p-bow-hands finger pose on every bow lower-body role.

## Landing selection (proposed)

The only records into a landing are jump-long's idle request (jump-long-land-idle) and the out-of-scope wall-jump-fail-45 / -90 fall requests (fall-land-soft-idle); every other landing has no incoming record, so the game code picks the landing and the rule is proposed. Band by vertical impact speed: soft < medium_impact_mps (about a 1.8 m drop) <= medium < hard_impact_mps (about 3.3 m) <= hard. Direction at touchdown: idle when not moving, run when moving with |move_dir_deg| <= thresholds.hemi_enter_deg, run_bw when moving further back (the graph's own backward landings, which exit into run-bkwd). A jump landing (last jump within jump-long + jump_land_window_s) in the soft or medium band uses the jump-long-land states; a hard impact always uses the fall table. Frontier's flat jump lands at about 4.4 m/s, so it takes the soft jump row.

Bands: soft < 6.0 m/s (1.84 m drop) <= medium < 8.0 m/s (3.27 m) <= hard; jump window 0.25 s after jump-long; backward when |move_dir_deg| > 90.0.

| kind/band | idle | idle_early | run | run_bw |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| fall/soft | land_soft_idle (fall-land-soft-idle: actor_b-p-land-soft^stealth-idle) | - | land_soft_run (fall-land-soft-run: actor_b-p-land-soft^run) | land_soft_run_bw (fall-land-soft-run-bw: actor_b-p-land-soft^run-bw) |
| fall/medium | land_medium_idle (fall-land-medium-idle: actor_b-p-land-med^idle) | - | land_medium_run (fall-land-medium-run: actor_b-p-land-med^run) | land_medium_run_bw (fall-land-medium-run-bw: actor_b-p-land-med^run-bw) |
| fall/hard | land_hard_idle (fall-land-hard-idle: actor_b-p-land-hard^idle) | - | land_hard_run (fall-land-hard-run: actor_b-p-land-hard^run) | land_hard_run_bw (fall-land-hard-run-bw: actor_b-p-land-hard^run-bw) |
| jump/soft | land_jump_idle (jump-long-land-idle: actor_b-p-land-hard^idle) | land_jump_idle_med (jump-long-land-idle-med: actor_b-p-land-med^idle) | land_jump_run (jump-long-land-run: actor_b-p-land-med^run) | land_soft_run_bw (fall-land-soft-run-bw: actor_b-p-land-soft^run-bw) |
| jump/medium | land_jump_idle (jump-long-land-idle: actor_b-p-land-hard^idle) | land_jump_idle_med (jump-long-land-idle-med: actor_b-p-land-med^idle) | land_jump_run_hard (jump-long-land-run-hard: MISSING -> land_jump_run) | land_medium_run_bw (fall-land-medium-run-bw: actor_b-p-land-med^run-bw) |

Clips shown for musket/run; every weapon x mode has its own resolved role. Fallbacks: land_jump_run_hard -> land_jump_run. Never selected: land_damage_idle (fall-land-damage-idle (land-hard-damage^idle): fall damage; Frontier has none. Exported with its exits for completeness); land_death (fall-land-death (land-hard-damage^death): lethal fall; Frontier has none. Exported with its exits for completeness).

- jump idle: jump-long-land-idle (land-hard^idle) is the target of jump-long's only authored landing record (request idle at phase >= 0.9, jump_to_land_idle), so it is the idle jump landing whenever that record could fire. Its sibling jump-long-land-idle-med (land-med^idle, same exits) is taken when the touchdown comes before phase 0.9, a short jump onto higher ground where the authored record cannot fire (INFERRED).
- jump run: jump-long-land-run (land-med^run) for the soft band, its heavier sibling jump-long-land-run-hard (land-hard^run-stumble) for the medium band, by name (INFERRED). The hard sibling has no Actor_B clip, so landing.fallback plays jump-long-land-run.
- run_bw: the jump landings have no backward variant; the graph's backward landings are the fall-land-*-run-bw states, which exit into run-bkwd (0x0002dee0, 0x0002de80 / 0x0002deb0). Without them a backpedal landing would play a forward clip and exit into run-fw

Graph landing states not exported:

- `dive-jump^land`: swim dive landing; out of scope
- `fall-land-partial`: partial-body landing (land-hard^idle) that only re-enters itself (0x00021330); no incoming record and no known trigger
- `jump-in-place-land`: landing of the in-place jump family (jump-in-place); Frontier's jump is the running long jump (run-jump-long)
- `jump-long-land-idle-no-stumble`: land-hard^idle with a single 0.3 s idle exit at phase >= 0.7 (0x0003e490) and no move exits; no incoming record and no known trigger

## Gameplay overrides (all default off)

- `override_crouch_crossfade` (CROUCH, default false): Plain 0.25 s stand/crouch crossfade without the graph's full-body crouch-change state (substitute clip) and without its crouch-change additive layer.
- `override_exact_hands_grip` (HANDS, default false): Drive finger/palm/prop bones from the grip clip (grips.<w>: carry, aim_stand, aim_crouch) at weight 1 in every carry and aim pose (runtime H node), replacing the graph's hands layers (e.g. 0.9 on musket locomotion), the move-set finger layers and the weapon-out finger partial, so the weapon always sits exactly in the authored grip.
- `override_fall_fade` (PREFALL, default false): 0.3 s into the fall loop instead of pre-fall's 0.5 s fade (the pre-fall clip itself is missing).
- `override_fast_aim_to_move` (AIM, default false): 0.3 s from aim idle into aimed movement instead of the graph's 0.5 s.
- `override_fast_hemisphere_fade` (G2, default false): 0.3 s hemisphere crossfade instead of the graph's 0.75 s: less lag on strafe reversals.
- `override_immediate_land_move` (G6, default false): Movement input interrupts a landing at once instead of after the graph's m2 >= 10 gate.
- `override_no_idle_turns` (G5, default false): Body yaw follows the camera with no turn-in-place clips.
- `override_no_transition_anims` (G1, default false): Skip start, stop and quick-reversal clips (the graph's own use_transition_anims = false branch): snappier response.
- `override_throw_windup_raise` (THROW, default false): Frontier-only wind-up for the thrown weapons: while one winds up, the graph's raise clip (throw-object-aim, 1h-aim-throw^aim) plays on the upper body seeked by Weapon.charge_amount(), so the arm draws back into the cocked aim pose as power builds, and hands over to the aim tree at full charge over throw-object-aim^aim's 0.2 s; a throw released while moving before the raise has finished plays the rest of it first (throw_raise_from_aimrun, then throw_raise_to_start). The graph has no charge field: its aim-in is the aim-idle crossfade (the aim tree weighted by Weapon.aim, the default), and it plays this raise only from aim-run.

## Tuning (no graph record)

Status and reason of every tuning value (none is a graph record): proposed = a Frontier value where the graph has no record (code-entered state, runtime mapping); approximation = stands in for graph behaviour the decoded data does not give; frontier = a Frontier-only feature the graph does not have. The report lists them (Tuning).

- `aim_stance_on` = 0.5 (proposed): Weapon.aim above which the lower body takes the aim-idle stance (plan risk 14)
- `aim_walk_max_speed_mps` = 1.0 (proposed): target-speed limit of the optional aim-walk bank (options.aim_walk, off)
- `blend_angle_rate` = 12.0 (proposed): smoothing rate (1/s) of the blend move angle f32@C8+4 on the circle (plan section 7)
- `blocked_len_m` = {"bow": 0.9, "musket": 1.5, "pistol": 0.6} (proposed): chest-ray length per weapon for the graph's weapon-blocked weight C8+172 = clamp((L - hit) / (0.5 L)) (plan section 7)
- `blocked_smooth_s` = 0.1 (proposed): smoothing of the blocked weight C8+172
- `carry_join_s` = 0.25 (approximation): at the graph's fade-0 lower joins (e.g. take-off -> jump, record 0x00026ab0) the base clip cuts as authored; the carry-layer stacks of the two states differ (musket take-off has no rifle-run-arms layer, the jump has) and the decoded layer records give weights only, not how the layers change at such a cut. The incoming layers ramp from the outgoing amounts over this long; PlayerAnim.strict_graph_cuts (off) cuts them with the base clip, the literal reading, which snaps the musket's left wrist at about 112 rad/s
- `charge_fire_fade` = [0.08, 0.2] (frontier): fade of the bow's fire additives: the held draw (a Frontier-only layer) hands over to the fire clip during its fade-in (bow-fire--add at 0 is 0.7 rad from the held draw at the drawing shoulder, 2.2 rad from the bind pose)
- `charge_let_down_s` = 0.25 (frontier): a let-down (the draw drops without a shot) unwinds the Frontier-only held-draw layer over this long instead of in one frame
- `chest_height_fallback` = {"crouch": 0.95, "stand": 1.35} (proposed): chest height (m) for the aim elevation when the metrics table has none (measured values come from actor_b_clip_metrics.json)
- `coyote_s` = 0.15 (approximation): grounded grace before the fall state; stands in for pre-fall, whose clip #ab73793b is missing (plan section 11)
- `dir_from_velocity_above` = 0.3 (proposed): the move direction (f32@C0+144) comes from the velocity above this speed, else from the stick (plan section 7)
- `fire_fade` = [0.02, 0.1] (proposed): fade in / out of the fire additives: the graph's fire state is entered by code with no transition record; the fire clip's first frame is already the recoil
- `grip_fade_s` = 0.15 (proposed): fade of the exact-grip override (override_exact_hands_grip) on and off
- `hit_add_fade` = [0.0, 0.2] (proposed): fade in / out of the light-hit additive (hit-reaction-par is entered by code; its exit record 0x000323b0 cuts at phase 0.9 with fade 0, so the fade-out ends by then)
- `mode_split_hysteresis_mps` = 0.1 (proposed): hysteresis around the walk / run split sqrt(v_walk * v_run) when the mode comes from the target speed (plan D9, G3)
- `moving_off_speed` = 0.6 (proposed): moving off below this ground speed with no stick input (m/s; plan section 7)
- `moving_on_speed` = 0.35 (proposed): moving on above this ground speed (m/s; plan section 7)
- `moving_on_wish` = 0.05 (proposed): the graph's move-speed field f32@C0+120 (> 0) mapped to Frontier input: moving on above this stick magnitude (plan section 7)
- `oneshot_fade` = [0.2, 0.15] (proposed): fade in / out of the action partials and their twins (draw, holster, nock, reloads): the graph's action states are entered by code with no transition record. Fade-in raised from 0.1 to 0.2 s in the WP6 review: at 0.1 s the crossfade from the carry pose into an action's first pose turned the arms at 40-58 rad/s (test_continuity storm, 26 + 12 frames)
- `pitch_smooth_s` = 0.03 (proposed): critically damped filter on the aim elevation that drives the aim up / down additives C8+248 / C8+252
- `rate_clamp` = [0.55, 1.75] (proposed): clamp of the locomotion playback rate = speed / blended stride on normalized timelines (plan D9), in place of the graph's calc-new-phase-from-speed callback
- `rate_floor_decel` = 0.6 (proposed): lowest playback rate while decelerating (plan D9 / test_rates)
- `stance_fade_s` = 0.25 (proposed): crossfade of the upper-body stance weights (grips, aim tree) on a crouch toggle
- `throw_release_timeout_s` = 0.35 (frontier): Throwable.anim_release_timeout while the animation drives the release (Throwable.wait_for_anim_release): the weapon's timer fallback past release_time when no release event came (the running throw's raise remainder delays the event)
- `throw_windup_fade_s` = 0.2 (frontier): the thrown weapons' wind-up raise (Frontier-only, options.throw_windup_raise; off by default, on only through gameplay_overrides.override_throw_windup_raise): the raise clip throw-object-aim, seeked by Weapon.charge_amount() like the bow's held draw, fades in over this long and hands over to the aim tree at full charge over the graph's throw-object-aim^aim -> aim-idle fade
- `upper_suppress_fade_s` = [0.1, 0.2] (proposed): the upper aim weight fades to 0 in / back from a heavy hit or death over these times (plan G4)
- `weapon_fade_s` = 0.25 (proposed): crossfade of the carry layers, grips and aim tree on a weapon change (the graph holsters and draws instead; the aim tree's own change record 0x00038070 uses 0.25 s)

## Proposed values (no graph record)

- `air_to_land`: fade_s=0.1 (the landings' only incoming records are jump-long's 0.1 s idle request (jump_to_land_idle) and the out-of-scope wall-jump-fail-45 / -90 fall requests into fall-land-soft-idle (also 0.1 s); the landing kind is chosen by the landing rule)
- `any_to_death`: fade_s=0.2 (death-front-mid has no incoming record (code-driven))
- `any_to_hit`: fade_s=0.1 (hit-reaction-full-body has no incoming record (code-driven))
- `crouch_to_takeoff`: fade_s=0.2 (Frontier-only: the player stands up into a jump from a crouch and the graph has no crouched jump; with ground_to_takeoff's 0.1 s the crouch pose and the musket's crouch arms swung into the take-off at up to 27 rad/s beyond the clips' own motion (test_continuity). The take-off -> jump join then fades over the rest of this fade (fade guard))
- `ground_to_takeoff`: fade_s=0.1 (run-jump-long has no incoming record (code-driven); the takeoff clip lasts 0.1 s)
- `idle_to_aimidle`: fade_s=0.2 (no incoming record into aim-idle from locomotion; all 38 'aim' request records use 0.2)
- `jump_to_fall`: fade_s=0.2 (jump-long has only the idle-request exit)
- landing kind: The only records into a landing are jump-long's idle request (jump-long-land-idle) and the out-of-scope wall-jump-fail-45 / -90 fall requests (fall-land-soft-idle); every other landing has no incoming record, so the game code picks the landing and the rule is proposed. Band by vertical impact speed: soft < medium_impact_mps (about a 1.8 m drop) <= medium < hard_impact_mps (about 3.3 m) <= hard. Direction at touchdown: idle when not moving, run when moving with |move_dir_deg| <= thresholds.hemi_enter_deg, run_bw when moving further back (the graph's own backward landings, which exit into run-bkwd). A jump landing (last jump within jump-long + jump_land_window_s) in the soft or medium band uses the jump-long-land states; a hard impact always uses the fall table. Frontier's flat jump lands at about 4.4 m/s, so it takes the soft jump row.

Frontier's Weapon.relaxed / Weapon.clips tables are not used by the graph-driven PlayerAnim; this compares them with the graph-resolved clips. relaxed_table_diff.match is true when Frontier's single [clip, weight] equals the graph's only partial arms layer (graph additives are listed). clip_table_diff.frontier_clip_evidence lists the overlay records that produce Frontier's clip where it differs from the graph's choice for that state.

## Warnings

- (a') musket/crouch bank rev_bw_fw: the direction leaves carry different carry layers (graph data; a runtime bank for this state needs per-leaf layer stacks)
- (a') musket/walk bank rev_fw_bw: the direction leaves carry different carry layers (graph data; a runtime bank for this state needs per-leaf layer stacks)
- (a') musket/walk bank start_bw: the direction leaves carry different carry layers (graph data; a runtime bank for this state needs per-leaf layer stacks)

- (a') pistol/walk bank rev_fw_bw: the direction leaves carry different carry layers (graph data; a runtime bank for this state needs per-leaf layer stacks)
- (a') pistol/walk bank start_bw: the direction leaves carry different carry layers (graph data; a runtime bank for this state needs per-leaf layer stacks)
